Please help [improve this section](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special\:EditPage/Beijing) by [adding citations to reliable sources](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Help\:Referencing_for_beginners). Unsourced material may be challenged and [removed](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia\:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence). *(December 2024)*_&#x20;([Learn how and when to remove this message](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Help\:Maintenance_template_removal))_ |
| --- | --- |



[KJ-2000](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/KJ-2000) and [J-10s](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chengdu_J-10) started the [flypast](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flypast) formation for the [70th anniversary of the People's Republic of China](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/70th_anniversary_of_the_People's_Republic_of_China).

The command headquarters of China's military forces are based in Beijing. The [Central Military Commission](214345.md), the political organ in charge of the military, is housed inside the [Ministry of National Defense](33632648.md), located next to the [Military Museum of the Chinese People's Revolution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Military_Museum_of_the_Chinese_People's_Revolution) in western Beijing. The [Rocket Force](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/People's_Liberation_Army_Rocket_Force), which controls the country's [strategic missile](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Intercontinental_ballistic_missile) and nuclear weapons, has its command in [Qinghe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Qinghe_Subdistrict,_Beijing), [Haidian District](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Haidian,_Beijing). The headquarters of the [Central Theater Command](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Central_Theater_Command), one of five nationally, is based further west in Gaojing. The CTR oversees the Beijing Capital Garrison as well as the [81st](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/81st_Army_\(People's_Republic_of_China\)), [82nd](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/82nd_Army_\(People's_Republic_of_China\)) and [83rd Armies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/83rd_Army_\(People's_Republic_of_China\)), which are based in [Hebei](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebei).

Military institutions in Beijing also include academies and [think tanks](37101.md) such as the [PLA National Defence University](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PLA_National_Defence_University) and [Academy of Military Science](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PLA_Academy_of_Military_Science), military hospitals such as the [301](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/301_Military_Hospital), [307](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/307_Hospital) and the Academy of Military Medical Sciences, and army-affiliated cultural entities such as 1 August Film Studios and the [PLA Song and Dance Troupe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chinese_People's_Liberation_Army_Naval_Song_and_Dance_Troupe).

The [China National Space Administration](344618.md), which oversees country's [space program](343674.md), and several space-related state owned companies, such as [CASTC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/China_Aerospace_Science_and_Technology_Corporation) and [CASIC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/China_Aerospace_Science_and_Industry_Corporation), are all based in Beijing. The [Beijing Aerospace Command and Control Center](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beijing_Aerospace_Command_and_Control_Center), in Haidian District, tracks the country's [crewed](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shenzhou_program) and uncrewed flight and other space exploration initiatives.

## Ecology



Wetland park in [Changping District](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Changping_District) in winter

Beijing's municipal territory encompasses a diverse range of ecosystems, including montane forests in the west and north, plains, wetlands, and river valleys. As of 2022, the city had achieved a forest coverage rate of 44.8 percent and an urban green coverage rate of 49.3 percent. Forest coverage has expanded dramatically since 1949, when it stood at just 1.3 percent. A biodiversity survey conducted between 2020 and 2024 documented 7,121 species across multiple categories, including higher plants, vertebrates, insects, and macro fungi, within 151 natural and semi-natural ecosystem types. The city is home to 608 species of wild terrestrial vertebrates and 2,088 species of plants, and hosts 515 species of wild birds, an increase of nearly 100 species over the preceding decade. Among the capitals of G20 nations, Beijing ranks second in bird species diversity.[^cite_note-276]

Beijing Municipality has 20 nature reserves, with a combined area of 1,339.7 km<sup>2</sup> (517.3 sq mi).[^cite_note-277] The mountains to the west and north of the city are home to a number of protected wildlife species including [leopard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Leopard), [leopard cat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Leopard_cat), [wolf](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wolf), [red fox](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_fox), [wild boar](52255.md), [masked palm civet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Masked_palm_civet), [raccoon dog](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Raccoon_dog), [hog badger](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hog_badger), [Siberian weasel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Siberian_weasel), [Amur hedgehog](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amur_hedgehog), [roe deer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roe_deer), and [mandarin rat snake](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mandarin_rat_snake).[^cite_note-278][^cite_note-279][^cite_note-280] The Beijing Aquatic Wildlife Rescue and Conservation Center protects the [Chinese giant salamander](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chinese_giant_salamander), [Amur stickleback](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amur_stickleback), and [mandarin duck](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mandarin_duck) on the Huaijiu and Huaisha Rivers in [Huairou District](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Huairou_District).[^cite_note-281] To the south of the city, the [Beijing Milu Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Milu_Yuan) is home to one of the largest herds of [Père David's deer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/P%C3%A8re_David's_deer), which are now extinct in the wild. [Endemic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Endemic) to Beijing is the [Beijing barbastelle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beijing_barbastelle), a species of [vesper bat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vesper_bat) discovered in caves of [Fangshan District](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fangshan_District) in 2001 and identified as a distinct species in 2007. The mountains of Fangshan are also the natural habitat for the more common [Beijing mouse-eared bat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beijing_mouse-eared_bat), [large myotis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Large_myotis), [greater horseshoe bat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Greater_horseshoe_bat), and [Rickett's big-footed bat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rickett's_big-footed_bat).[^cite_note-282]

&#x20;Each year, Beijing hosts 200–300 species of [migratory birds](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Migratory_birds) including the [common crane](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Common_crane), [black-headed gull](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Black-headed_gull), [swan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Swan), [mallard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mallard), [common cuckoo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Common_cuckoo), and the endangered [yellow-breasted bunting](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yellow-breasted_bunting).[^cite_note-283][^cite_note-BJ_Evening_News_2016-10-30-284] In May 2016, Common cuckoos nesting in the wetlands of Cuihu (Haidian), Hanshiqiao (Shunyi), Yeyahu (Yanqing) were tagged and have been traced as far as India, Kenya and [Mozambique](19301.md).[^cite_note-285][^cite_note-286] In the fall of 2016, the Beijing Forest Police undertook a month-long campaign to crack down on illegal hunting and trapping of migratory birds for sale in local bird markets.[^cite_note-BJ_Evening_News_2016-10-30-284] Over 1,000 rescued birds of protected species including [streptopelia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Streptopelia), [Eurasian siskin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eurasian_siskin), [crested myna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Crested_myna), [coal tit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Coal_tit), and [great tit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Great_tit) were handed to the Beijing Wildlife Protection and Rescue Center for repatriation to the wild.[^cite_note-BJ_Evening_News_2016-10-30-284][^cite_note-287]



Père David's deer (*Elaphurus davidianus*) at Beijing Milu Park, Nanyuan.

Beijing has a particular historical association with Père David's deer (*Elaphurus davidianus*), a species known in Chinese as *sibuxiang* ("four-unlikes"), which survived extinction only because specimens from the imperial hunting park at Nanyuan were brought to Europe in the 19th century; the species was reintroduced to Beijing in 1985 after becoming extinct in China during the Boxer Rebellion of 1900.[^cite_note-288]

The city flowers are the [Chinese rose](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rosa_chinensis) and [chrysanthemum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chrysanthemum).[^cite_note-cityflowertree-289] The city trees are the [Chinese arborvitae](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chinese_arborvitae) (an [evergreen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Evergreen) in the [cypress family](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cypress_family)) and the [pagoda tree](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pagoda_tree) (a [deciduous](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deciduous) tree in the [bean family](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bean_family), also called the Chinese scholar tree).[^cite_note-cityflowertree-289] The oldest scholar tree in the city was planted in what is now [Beihai Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beihai_Park) during the [Tang dynasty](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tang_dynasty).[^cite_note-290]

## International relations

The capital is the home of the [Asian Infrastructure Investment Bank](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Asian_Infrastructure_Investment_Bank), a [multilateral development bank](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multilateral_development_bank) that aims to improve economic and social outcomes in Asia[^cite_note-291] and the [Silk Road Fund](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Silk_Road_Fund), an investment fund of the Chinese government to foster increased investment and provide financial supports in countries along the [One Belt, One Road](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/One_Belt,_One_Road).[^cite_note-292] Beijing is also home to the headquarters of the [Shanghai Cooperation Organisation](152422.md) (SCO),[^cite_note-293] making it an important city for [international diplomacy](28165911.md).

### Twin towns and sister cities

See also: [List of twin towns and sister cities in China](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_twin_towns_and_sister_cities_in_China)

Beijing is [twinned](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sister_city) with the following regions, cities, and counties:[^cite_note-294]

-  [Addis Ababa](52593.md), Ethiopia

-  [Ankara](802.md), Turkey

-  [Astana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Astana), Kazakhstan

-  [Athens](1216.md), Greece

-  [Bangkok](56615.md), Thailand

-  [Berlin](3354.md), Germany

-  [Brussels](3708.md), Belgium

### Foreign embassies and consulates

See also: [List of diplomatic missions in China](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_diplomatic_missions_in_China)

In 2019, China had the largest [diplomatic network](8970.md) in the world.[^cite_note-295] China hosts a [large diplomatic community](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_diplomatic_missions_in_China) in its capital city of Beijing; as of 2020, the Chinese capital hosts 173 embassies, one consulate and three representatives, excluding the [trade offices](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trade_office) of [Hong Kong](13404.md) and [Macau](19068.md).[^cite_note-296][^cite_note-297]
